Result: 在HPatches、ScanNet、IMC2022和Aachen数据集上的实验表明，AsymLoc使用小一个数量级的模型达到了教师模型95%的定位精度，显著优于现有基线，建立了新的效率-精度权衡标准

Conclusion: AsymLoc通过创新的非对称蒸馏方法，在资源受限设备上实现了高效准确的视觉定位，为边缘计算应用提供了实用的解决方案

Abstract: Precise and real-time visual localization is critical for applications like AR/VR and robotics, especially on resource-constrained edge devices such as smart glasses, where battery life and heat dissipation can be a primary concerns. While many efficient models exist, further reducing compute without sacrificing accuracy is essential for practical deployment. To address this, we propose asymmetric visual localization: a large Teacher model processes pre-mapped database images offline, while a lightweight Student model processes the query image online. This creates a challenge in matching features from two different models without resorting to heavy, learned matchers.
  We introduce AsymLoc, a novel distillation framework that aligns a Student to its Teacher through a combination of a geometry-driven matching objective and a joint detector-descriptor distillation objective, enabling fast, parameter-less nearest-neighbor matching. Extensive experiments on HPatches, ScanNet, IMC2022, and Aachen show that AsymLoc achieves up to 95% of the teacher's localization accuracy using an order of magnitude smaller models, significantly outperforming existing baselines and establishing a new state-of-the-art efficiency-accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [115] [Realizing Immersive Volumetric Video: A Multimodal Framework for 6-DoF VR Engagement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09473)
*Zhengxian Yang,Shengqi Wang,Shi Pan,Hongshuai Li,Haoxiang Wang,Lin Li,Guanjun Li,Zhengqi Wen,Borong Lin,Jianhua Tao,Tao Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Immersive Volumetric Videos新格式，构建ImViD多模态数据集，开发动态光场重建和声场重建方法，形成沉浸式体视频生产统一流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟现实需要6-DoF视觉听觉交互的沉浸式体验，但直接从真实世界视频构建这种体验仍未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 构建ImViD多视角多模态数据集，采用空间导向捕获理念；2) 基于高斯时空表示开发动态光场重建框架；3) 提出首个从多视角视听数据重建声场的方法。

Result: 生成高质量、时间稳定的视听体内容，提供大6-DoF交互空间，在VR实验中表现优异，超越现有基准。

Conclusion: 为沉浸式体视频提供了基础定义和实际构建方法，推动了真实世界捕获的沉浸式体验发展。

Abstract: Fully immersive experiences that tightly integrate 6-DoF visual and auditory interaction are essential for virtual and augmented reality. While such experiences can be achieved through computer-generated content, constructing them directly from real-world captured videos remains largely unexplored. We introduce Immersive Volumetric Videos, a new volumetric media format designed to provide large 6-DoF interaction spaces, audiovisual feedback, and high-resolution, high-frame-rate dynamic content. To support IVV construction, we present ImViD, a multi-view, multi-modal dataset built upon a space-oriented capture philosophy. Our custom capture rig enables synchronized multi-view video-audio acquisition during motion, facilitating efficient capture of complex indoor and outdoor scenes with rich foreground--background interactions and challenging dynamics. The dataset provides 5K-resolution videos at 60 FPS with durations of 1-5 minutes, offering richer spatial, temporal, and multimodal coverage than existing benchmarks. Leveraging this dataset, we develop a dynamic light field reconstruction framework built upon a Gaussian-based spatio-temporal representation, incorporating flow-guided sparse initialization, joint camera temporal calibration, and multi-term spatio-temporal supervision for robust and accurate modeling of complex motion. We further propose, to our knowledge, the first method for sound field reconstruction from such multi-view audiovisual data. Together, these components form a unified pipeline for immersive volumetric video production. Extensive benchmarks and immersive VR experiments demonstrate that our pipeline generates high-quality, temporally stable audiovisual volumetric content with large 6-DoF interaction spaces. This work provides both a foundational definition and a practical construction methodology for immersive volumetric videos.

</details>


### [116] [Online3R: Online Learning for Consistent Sequential Reconstruction Based on Geometry Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09480)
*Shunkai Zhou,Zike Yan,Fei Xue,Dong Wu,Yuchen Deng,Hongbin Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Online3R：一种通过在线学习适应新场景的顺序重建框架，使用轻量级视觉提示和局部-全局自监督学习解决不一致性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在新场景中面临不一致性问题，需要能够适应新环境同时保持基础模型几何预测能力的解决方案

Method: 在预训练冻结的几何基础模型中引入可学习的轻量级视觉提示，采用局部-全局自监督学习策略：局部一致性约束在中间和先前局部融合结果上执行，全局一致性约束在稀疏关键帧上操作

Result: Online3R在多个基准测试中优于先前的最先进方法

Conclusion: 该框架通过在线学习有效解决不一致性问题，能够适应新场景同时保持基础模型的几何预测能力

Abstract: We present Online3R, a new sequential reconstruction framework that is capable of adapting to new scenes through online learning, effectively resolving inconsistency issues. Specifically, we introduce a set of learnable lightweight visual prompts into a pretrained, frozen geometry foundation model to capture the knowledge of new environments while preserving the fundamental capability of the foundation model for geometry prediction. To solve the problems of missing groundtruth and the requirement of high efficiency when updating these visual prompts at test time, we introduce a local-global self-supervised learning strategy by enforcing the local and global consistency constraints on predictions. The local consistency constraints are conducted on intermediate and previously local fused results, enabling the model to be trained with high-quality pseudo groundtruth signals; the global consistency constraints are operated on sparse keyframes spanning long distances rather than per frame, allowing the model to learn from a consistent prediction over a long trajectory in an efficient way. Our experiments demonstrate that Online3R outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods on various benchmarks. Project page: https://shunkaizhou.github.io/online3r-1.0/

</details>


### [117] [RIRF: Reasoning Image Restoration Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09511)
*Wending Yan,Rongkai Zhang,Kaihua Tang,Yu Cheng,Qiankun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R&R是一个新颖的通用图像修复框架，通过集成结构化思维链推理来诊断图像退化类型和严重程度，为修复过程提供可解释的指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用图像修复方法主要关注像素重建，缺乏对退化组成、严重程度和场景语义的显式诊断推理。需要将语义诊断推理与像素级修复紧密结合。

Method: 提出Reason and Restore (R&R)框架，包含一个基于Qwen3-VL微调实现的推理器，用于诊断退化类型、量化严重程度、推断关键因素和描述场景语义。利用量化退化严重程度作为强化学习信号指导修复器。

Result: 在多个通用图像修复基准测试中，R&R实现了最先进的性能，同时为修复过程提供了独特的可解释性。

Conclusion: R&R通过将结构化思维链推理集成到图像修复流程中，实现了语义诊断推理与像素级修复的紧密耦合，在保持高性能的同时提供了可解释的修复过程。

Abstract: Universal image restoration (UIR) aims to recover clean images from diverse and unknown degradations using a unified model. Existing UIR methods primarily focus on pixel reconstruction and often lack explicit diagnostic reasoning over degradation composition, severity, and scene semantics prior to restoration. We propose Reason and Restore (R\&R), a novel framework that integrates structured Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning into the image restoration pipeline. R\&R introduces an explicit reasoner, implemented by fine-tuning Qwen3-VL, to diagnose degradation types, quantify degradation severity, infer key degradation-related factors, and describe relevant scene and object semantics. The resulting structured reasoning provides interpretable and fine-grained diagnostic priors for the restorer. To further improve restoration quality, the quantified degradation severity produced by the reasoner is leveraged as reinforcement learning (RL) signals to guide and strengthen the restorer. Unlike existing multimodal LLM-based agentic systems that decouple reasoning from low-level vision tasks, R\&R tightly couples semantic diagnostic reasoning with pixel-level restoration in a unified framework. Extensive experiments across diverse UIR benchmarks demonstrate that R\&R achieves state-of-the-art performance while offering unique interpretability into the restoration process.

</details>


### [118] [EgoTL: Egocentric Think-Aloud Chains for Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09535)
*Lulin Liu,Dayou Li,Yiqing Liang,Sicong Jiang,Hitesh Vijay,Hezhen Hu,Xuhai Xu,Zirui Liu,Srinivas Shakkottai,Manling Li,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoTL：通过"先思考后行动"协议构建具身智能的思维过程数据集，解决VLM在长时程空间指令跟随中的噪声标注问题，提升基础模型在家庭任务中的规划与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM自动标注存在噪声问题，主要源于缺乏准确的人类动作标签、思维链和空间标注，导致在长时程空间指令跟随中错误被放大。这些问题源于对分钟级日常家庭规划任务的覆盖不足和空间定位不准确。

Method: EgoTL构建"先思考后行动"的思维过程捕捉流程，记录逐步目标和带时间戳的口头推理，通过度量级空间估计器校准物理属性，使用记忆库浏览获取场景上下文，并为导航指令和详细操作动作添加片段级标签。

Result: 在100多个日常家庭任务中，从三个层次的六个任务维度对VLM和世界模型进行基准测试，发现基础模型仍不足以作为具身助手或开放世界模拟器。但使用EgoTL训练数据微调基础模型后，显著改善了长时程规划推理、逐步推理、指令跟随和空间定位能力。

Conclusion: EgoTL通过系统化的思维过程捕捉和空间校准，为具身智能提供了高质量的标注数据，有效解决了VLM在长时程家庭任务中的噪声问题，显著提升了基础模型在复杂现实场景中的规划与推理能力。

Abstract: Large foundation models have made significant advances in embodied intelligence, enabling synthesis and reasoning over egocentric input for household tasks. However, VLM-based auto-labeling is often noisy because the primary data sources lack accurate human action labels, chain-of-thought (CoT), and spatial annotations; these errors are amplified during long-horizon spatial instruction following. These issues stem from insufficient coverage of minute-long, daily household planning tasks and from inaccurate spatial grounding. As a result, VLM reasoning chains and world-model synthesis can hallucinate objects, skip steps, or fail to respect real-world physical attributes. To address these gaps, we introduce EgoTL. EgoTL builds a think-aloud capture pipeline for egocentric data. It uses a say-before-act protocol to record step-by-step goals and spoken reasoning with word-level timestamps, then calibrates physical properties with metric-scale spatial estimators, a memory-bank walkthrough for scene context, and clip-level tags for navigation instructions and detailed manipulation actions. With EgoTL, we are able to benchmark VLMs and World Models on six task dimensions from three layers and long-horizon generation over minute-long sequences across over 100 daily household tasks. We find that foundation models still fall short as egocentric assistants or open-world simulators. Finally, we finetune foundation models with human CoT aligned with metric labels on the training split of EgoTL, which improves long-horizon planning and reasoning, step-wise reasoning, instruction following, and spatial grounding.

</details>


### [119] [Tango: Taming Visual Signals for Efficient Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09547)
*Shukang Yin,Sirui Zhao,Hanchao Wang,Baozhi Jia,Xianquan Wang,Chaoyou Fu,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tango是一个优化视频大语言模型token剪枝的新框架，通过多样性驱动的注意力选择和时空旋转位置编码，在保留10% token时能保持98.9%性能并实现1.88倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型的token剪枝方法存在两个关键问题：1）传统的top-k选择策略未能充分考虑注意力分布的多模态和长尾特性；2）直接的相似性聚类常产生碎片化簇，导致池化后表示失真。

Method: 提出Tango框架，包含：1）多样性驱动的策略来增强基于注意力的token选择；2）时空旋转位置编码（ST-RoPE）通过局部先验保持几何结构。

Result: 在各种视频大语言模型和视频理解基准测试中验证了方法的有效性和泛化性。在LLaVA-OV上仅保留10%视频token时，能保持98.9%的原始性能，同时实现1.88倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: Tango通过优化视觉信号利用，解决了现有token剪枝方法的局限性，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理效率，为高效视频大语言模型开发提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Token pruning has emerged as a mainstream approach for developing efficient Video Large Language Models (Video LLMs). This work revisits and advances the two predominant token-pruning paradigms: attention-based selection and similarity-based clustering. Our study reveals two critical limitations in existing methods: (1) conventional top-k selection strategies fail to fully account for the attention distribution, which is often spatially multi-modal and long-tailed in magnitude; and (2) direct similarity-based clustering frequently generates fragmented clusters, resulting in distorted representations after pooling. To address these bottlenecks, we propose Tango, a novel framework designed to optimize the utilization of visual signals. Tango integrates a diversity-driven strategy to enhance attention-based token selection, and introduces Spatio-temporal Rotary Position Embedding (ST-RoPE) to preserve geometric structure via locality priors. Comprehensive experiments across various Video LLMs and video understanding benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and generalizability of our approach. Notably, when retaining only 10% of the video tokens, Tango preserves 98.9% of the original performance on LLaVA-OV while delivering a 1.88x inference speedup.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [LEGO: Latent-space Exploration for Geometry-aware Optimization of Humanoid Kinematic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08636)
*Jihwan Yoon,Taemoon Jeong,Jeongeun Park,Chanwoo Kim,Jaewoon Kwon,Yonghyeon Lee,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的机器人设计新范式，通过从现有机械设计中学习设计搜索空间，并利用人类运动数据定义损失函数，实现机器人形态与运动协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人形态学和运动学设计依赖人类直觉，缺乏系统化基础。运动设计协同优化面临两大挑战：庞大的非结构化设计空间和构建任务特定损失函数的困难。

Method: 使用基于螺旋理论的关节轴表示和等距流形学习，构建紧凑、几何保持的人形机器人上半身设计潜在空间；通过运动重定向和Procrustes分析从人类运动数据定义损失函数；在潜在空间中使用无梯度优化进行设计优化。

Result: 建立了一个数据驱动机器人设计的原理性框架，证明利用现有设计和人类运动可以有效指导新型机器人设计的自动化发现。

Conclusion: 提出的方法最小化人类参与，通过从现有机械设计学习设计搜索空间，直接从人类运动数据定义损失，为机器人形态与运动协同优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Designing robot morphologies and kinematics has traditionally relied on human intuition, with little systematic foundation. Motion-design co-optimization offers a promising path toward automation, but two major challenges remain: (i) the vast, unstructured design space and (ii) the difficulty of constructing task-specific loss functions. We propose a new paradigm that minimizes human involvement by (i) learning the design search space from existing mechanical designs, rather than hand-crafting it, and (ii) defining the loss directly from human motion data via motion retargeting and Procrustes analysis. Using screw-theory-based joint axis representation and isometric manifold learning, we construct a compact, geometry-preserving latent space of humanoid upper body designs in which optimization is tractable. We then solve design optimization in this latent space using gradient-free optimization. Our approach establishes a principled framework for data-driven robot design and demonstrates that leveraging existing designs and human motion can effectively guide the automated discovery of novel robot design.

</details>


### [121] [Generative Simulation for Policy Learning in Physical Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08664)
*Junxiang Wang,Xinwen Xu,Tiancheng Wu,Julian Millan,Nir Pechuk,Zackory Erickson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个用于物理人机交互的零样本"文本到仿真到现实"生成框架，通过自然语言提示自动生成多样化的pHRI场景，实现大规模合成数据收集和视觉模仿学习策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发自主物理人机交互系统面临大规模训练数据稀缺的挑战，难以学习适用于真实世界的鲁棒机器人行为。

Method: 利用大语言模型和视觉语言模型构建生成式仿真框架，从高层次自然语言提示自动生成软体人体模型、场景布局和机器人运动轨迹，收集大规模合成演示数据集，训练基于分割点云的视觉模仿学习策略。

Result: 在抓痒和洗澡两个辅助任务上，学习到的策略成功实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，成功率超过80%，并对非脚本化人体运动表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了首个用于pHRI应用的生成式仿真流水线，实现了仿真环境合成、数据收集和策略学习的自动化，为解决物理人机交互系统训练数据稀缺问题提供了创新解决方案。

Abstract: Developing autonomous physical human-robot interaction (pHRI) systems is limited by the scarcity of large-scale training data to learn robust robot behaviors for real-world applications. In this paper, we introduce a zero-shot "text2sim2real" generative simulation framework that automatically synthesizes diverse pHRI scenarios from high-level natural-language prompts. Leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), our pipeline procedurally generates soft-body human models, scene layouts, and robot motion trajectories for assistive tasks. We utilize this framework to autonomously collect large-scale synthetic demonstration datasets and then train vision-based imitation learning policies operating on segmented point clouds. We evaluate our approach through a user study on two physically assistive tasks: scratching and bathing. Our learned policies successfully achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, attaining success rates exceeding 80% and demonstrating resilience to unscripted human motion. Overall, we introduce the first generative simulation pipeline for pHRI applications, automating simulation environment synthesis, data collection, and policy learning. Additional information may be found on our project website: https://rchi-lab.github.io/gen_phri/

</details>


### [122] [Task-Aware Bimanual Affordance Prediction via VLM-Guided Semantic-Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08726)
*Fabian Hahne,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个分层框架，利用视觉语言模型进行任务感知的双臂操作可负担性预测，解决双臂操作中的可负担性定位和手臂分配联合问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作需要同时考虑与物体的交互位置和手臂分配，这是一个几何规划器无法单独解决的联合问题。现有方法要么将可负担性预测视为粗略的部分分割，要么依赖几何启发式进行手臂分配，未能联合推理任务相关的接触区域和手臂分配。

Method: 提出分层框架：1）融合多视角RGB-D观测到一致的3D场景表示；2）生成全局6-DoF抓取候选；3）查询视觉语言模型进行空间和语义过滤，获取每个对象上任务相关的可负担性区域和手臂分配；4）确保几何有效性同时尊重任务语义。

Result: 在双臂平台上评估了9个真实世界操作任务，涵盖4个类别：并行操作、协调稳定、工具使用和人类交接。相比几何和语义基线，该方法在任务导向抓取方面实现了更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 对可负担性和手臂分配的显式语义推理有助于在非结构化环境中实现可靠的双臂操作。该方法无需类别特定训练，能够跨对象类别和任务描述泛化。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation requires reasoning about where to interact with an object and which arm should perform each action, a joint affordance localization and arm allocation problem that geometry-only planners cannot resolve without semantic understanding of task intent. Existing approaches either treat affordance prediction as coarse part segmentation or rely on geometric heuristics for arm assignment, failing to jointly reason about task-relevant contact regions and arm allocation. We reframe bimanual manipulation as a joint affordance localization and arm allocation problem and propose a hierarchical framework for task-aware bimanual affordance prediction that leverages a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to generalize across object categories and task descriptions without requiring category-specific training. Our approach fuses multi-view RGB-D observations into a consistent 3D scene representation and generates global 6-DoF grasp candidates, which are then spatially and semantically filtered by querying the VLM for task-relevant affordance regions on each object, as well as for arm allocation to the individual objects, thereby ensuring geometric validity while respecting task semantics. We evaluate our method on a dual-arm platform across nine real-world manipulation tasks spanning four categories: parallel manipulation, coordinated stabilization, tool use, and human handover. Our approach achieves consistently higher task success rates than geometric and semantic baselines for task-oriented grasping, demonstrating that explicit semantic reasoning over affordances and arm allocation helps enable reliable bimanual manipulation in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [123] [Toward Hardware-Agnostic Quadrupedal World Models via Morphology Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08780)
*Mohamad H. Danesh,Chenhao Li,Amin Abyaneh,Anas Houssaini,Kirsty Ellis,Glen Berseth,Marco Hutter,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出QWM框架，通过显式编码机器人形态学参数，实现四足机器人世界模型在不同形态间的零样本泛化，解决现有模型硬件锁定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型存在硬件锁定问题：针对特定机器人训练的世界模型无法泛化到其他形态的机器人，因为模型过度拟合了特定硬件的运动学和动力学特性，而非学习通用的运动规律。微小的硬件变化就需要重新训练模型，效率低下。

Method: 提出Quadrupedal World Model (QWM)框架：1）显式地将机器人工程规格（形态学参数）作为生成动力学的条件输入，而非隐式地从运动历史推断；2）集成物理形态编码器和奖励归一化器；3）使模型能够作为神经模拟器在不同形态间泛化。

Result: 首次实现了能够在不同形态四足机器人间进行零样本泛化的世界模型。QWM能够作为分布有界的插值器，在四足机器人形态家族内实现泛化，解锁了跨形态的零样本控制能力。

Conclusion: 虽然QWM是四足机器人形态家族内的插值器而非通用物理引擎，但这项工作代表了向形态条件化世界模型迈出的重要一步，为解决机器人世界模型的硬件锁定问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: World models promise a paradigm shift in robotics, where an agent learns the underlying physics of its environment once to enable efficient planning and behavior learning. However, current world models are often hardware-locked specialists: a model trained on a Boston Dynamics Spot robot fails catastrophically on a Unitree Go1 due to the mismatch in kinematic and dynamic properties, as the model overfits to specific embodiment constraints rather than capturing the universal locomotion dynamics. Consequently, a slight change in actuator dynamics or limb length necessitates training a new model from scratch. In this work, we take a step towards a framework for training a generalizable Quadrupedal World Model (QWM) that disentangles environmental dynamics from robot morphology. We address the limitations of implicit system identification, where treating static physical properties (like mass or limb length) as latent variables to be inferred from motion history creates an adaptation lag that can compromise zero-shot safety and efficiency. Instead, we explicitly condition the generative dynamics on the robot's engineering specifications. By integrating a physical morphology encoder and a reward normalizer, we enable the model to serve as a neural simulator capable of generalizing across morphologies. This capability unlocks zero-shot control across a range of embodiments. We introduce, for the first time, a world model that enables zero-shot generalization to new morphologies for locomotion. While we carefully study the limitations of our method, QWM operates as a distribution-bounded interpolator within the quadrupedal morphology family rather than a universal physics engine, this work represents a significant step toward morphology-conditioned world models for legged locomotion.

</details>


### [124] [One Interface, Many Robots: Unified Real-Time Low-Level Motion Planning for Collaborative Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08787)
*Yue Feng,Weicheng Huang,I-Ming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于协作机械臂实时低级运动规划的通用接口，通过多项式插值和二次规划求解器生成平滑轨迹，并在离线绘图、动态抓取和遥操作三种场景中验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为协作机械臂的实时低级运动规划提供通用接口，提高在不同硬件平台上的适用性和可移植性，解决异构机器人系统间的兼容性问题

Method: 基于WinGs Operating Studio中间件，提供最小化但灵活的末端执行器轨迹控制接口，采用n次多项式插值器和二次规划求解器生成具有精确位置、速度和加速度轮廓的平滑可微轨迹

Result: 在三种场景中验证：1）离线演示中机械臂准确绘制几何图形；2）可中断的低频重规划场景中抓取移动机器人上的动态物体；3）遥操作实验中一个机械臂控制另一个执行灵巧操作，证实了方法的可靠性、多功能性和易用性

Conclusion: 提出的通用接口能够有效支持协作机械臂的实时低级运动规划，在不同应用场景中表现出良好的性能，为异构机器人系统的标准化控制提供了可行方案

Abstract: This paper proposes a common interface for real-time low-level motion planning of collaborative robotic arms, aimed at enabling broader applicability and improved portability across heterogeneous hardware platforms. In previous work, we introduced WinGs Operating Studio (WOS), a middleware solution that abstracts diverse robotic components into uniform software resources and provides a broad suite of language-agnostic APIs. This paper specifically focuses on its minimal yet flexible interface for real-time end-effector trajectory control. By employing an n-degree polynomial interpolator in conjunction with a quadratic programming solver, the proposed method generates smooth, continuously differentiable trajectories with precise position, velocity, and acceleration profiles. We validate our approach in three distinct scenarios. First, in an offline demonstration, a collaborative arm accurately draws various geometric shapes on paper. Second, in an interruptible, low-frequency re-planning setting, a robotic manipulator grasps a dynamic object placed on a moving mobile robot. Finally, we conducted a teleoperation experiment in which one robotic arm controlled another to perform a series of dexterous manipulations, confirming the proposed method's reliability, versatility, and ease of use.

</details>


### [125] [Simulation of Adaptive Running with Flexible Sports Prosthesis using Reinforcement Learning of Hybrid-link System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08882)
*Yuta Shimane,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的单侧胫骨截肢者跑步运动仿真方法，使用混合连杆系统模拟叶片弹簧型运动假肢的柔性变形，分析不同虚拟假肢刚度对跑步性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动假肢的设计和选择通常依赖试错法。需要一种考虑人体运动与假肢变形相互作用的全身动力学分析方法，为用户特定的设计和选择提供有价值的见解。

Method: 采用混合连杆系统，结合分段恒定应变模型表示假肢的柔性变形。基于强化学习的方法生成单侧胫骨截肢者的全身动态运动，结合运动捕捉数据的模仿学习和精确的假肢动力学计算。

Result: 模拟了不同虚拟假肢刚度条件下的跑步运动，分析了模拟中获得的代谢运输成本，表明刚度变化会影响跑步性能。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了在虚拟条件下进行仿真和分析的潜力，这些条件可能与实际情况不同，为运动假肢的设计和选择提供了新的工具。

Abstract: This study proposes a reinforcement learning-based adaptive running motion simulation for a unilateral transtibial amputee with the flexibility of a leaf-spring-type sports prosthesis using hybrid-link system. The design and selection of sports prostheses often rely on trial and error. A comprehensive whole-body dynamics analysis that considers the interaction between human motion and prosthetic deformation could provide valuable insights for user-specific design and selection. The hybrid-link system facilitates whole-body dynamics analysis by incorporating the Piece-wise Constant Strain model to represent the flexible deformation of the prosthesis. Based on this system, the simulation methodology generates whole-body dynamic motions of a unilateral transtibial amputee through a reinforcement learning-based approach, which combines imitation learning from motion capture data with accurate prosthetic dynamics computation. We simulated running motions under different virtual prosthetic stiffness conditions and analyzed the metabolic cost of transport obtained from the simulations, suggesting that variations in stiffness influence running performance. Our findings demonstrate the potential of this approach for simulation and analysis under virtual conditions that differ from real conditions.

</details>


### [126] [HTNav: A Hybrid Navigation Framework with Tiered Structure for Urban Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08883)
*Chengjie Fan,Cong Pan,Zijian Liu,Ningzhong Liu,Jie Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HTNav：一种结合模仿学习和强化学习的混合导航框架，用于解决复杂城市环境中空中视觉语言导航的泛化性、长距离路径规划和空间连续性理解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中视觉语言导航方法在复杂城市环境中面临三大挑战：1）对未见场景泛化能力不足；2）长距离路径规划性能欠佳；3）空间连续性理解不够深入。这些限制了其在物流配送、城市巡检等实际应用中的价值。

Method: 提出HTNav框架，采用混合IL-RL（模仿学习-强化学习）架构：1）分阶段训练机制确保基础导航策略稳定性同时增强环境探索能力；2）分层决策机制实现宏观路径规划与细粒度动作控制的协同交互；3）引入地图表示学习模块加深对开放域空间连续性的理解。

Result: 在CityNav基准测试中，该方法在所有场景级别和任务难度上均达到最先进性能。实验结果表明该框架显著提升了复杂城市环境中的导航精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HTNav通过混合IL-RL框架有效解决了空中视觉语言导航在复杂城市环境中的关键挑战，为实际应用提供了更可靠、更智能的导航解决方案。

Abstract: Inspired by the general Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) task, aerial VLN has attracted widespread attention, owing to its significant practical value in applications such as logistics delivery and urban inspection. However, existing methods face several challenges in complex urban environments, including insufficient generalization to unseen scenes, suboptimal performance in long-range path planning, and inadequate understanding of spatial continuity. To address these challenges, we propose HTNav, a new collaborative navigation framework that integrates Imitation Learning (IL) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) within a hybrid IL-RL framework. This framework adopts a staged training mechanism to ensure the stability of the basic navigation strategy while enhancing its environmental exploration capability. By integrating a tiered decision-making mechanism, it achieves collaborative interaction between macro-level path planning and fine-grained action control. Furthermore, a map representation learning module is introduced to deepen its understanding of spatial continuity in open domains. On the CityNav benchmark, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across all scene levels and task difficulties. Experimental results demonstrate that this framework significantly improves navigation precision and robustness in complex urban environments.

</details>


### [127] [AssemLM: Spatial Reasoning Multimodal Large Language Models for Robotic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08983)
*Zhi Jing,Jinbin Qiao,Ouyang Lu,Jicong Ao,Shuang Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AssemLM是一个面向机器人装配的空间多模态大语言模型，通过整合装配手册、点云和文本指令来推理6D装配姿态，实现了对3D几何的精确理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型主要依赖粗糙的2D感知，缺乏对3D几何的精确推理能力，而这是机器人精细装配任务所必需的。需要开发能够进行准确3D空间推理的模型来支持机器人装配应用。

Method: 提出AssemLM模型，采用专门的点云编码器捕获细粒度几何和旋转特征，然后将这些特征集成到多模态语言模型中。同时构建了AssemBench数据集，包含超过90万带精确6D姿态标注的多模态样本。

Result: AssemLM在各种装配场景的6D姿态推理中取得了最先进的性能。真实机器人评估表明，该模型能够支持现实世界中的细粒度多步装配执行。

Conclusion: AssemLM通过整合3D几何理解和多模态推理，显著提升了机器人装配的空间推理能力，填补了现有具身AI基准在3D几何推理方面的空白，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning is a fundamental capability for embodied intelligence, especially for fine-grained manipulation tasks such as robotic assembly. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit preliminary spatial awareness, they largely rely on coarse 2D perception and lack the ability to perform accurate reasoning over 3D geometry, which is crucial for precise assembly operations. To address this limitation, we propose AssemLM, a spatial multimodal large language model tailored for robotic assembly. AssemLM integrates assembly manuals, point clouds, and textual instructions to reason about and predict task-critical 6D assembly poses, enabling explicit geometric understanding throughout the assembly process. To effectively bridge raw 3D perception and high-level reasoning, we adopt a specialized point cloud encoder to capture fine-grained geometric and rotational features, which are then integrated into the multimodal language model to support accurate 3D spatial reasoning for assembly tasks. In addition, we construct AssemBench, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for assembly-oriented spatial reasoning, comprising over 900K multimodal samples with precise 6D pose annotations. AssemBench extends spatial reasoning evaluation beyond 2D and grounding tasks into full 3D geometric inference, filling a critical gap in existing embodied AI benchmarks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AssemLM achieves state-of-the-art performance in 6D pose reasoning across diverse assembly scenarios. Furthermore, real-robot evaluations show that our model can support fine-grained and multi-step assembly execution in real-world settings, demonstrating its potential for robotic assembly applications.

</details>


### [128] [V-CAGE: Vision-Closed-Loop Agentic Generation Engine for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09036)
*Yaru Liu,Ao-bo Wang,Nanyang Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V-CAGE是一个用于自主机器人数据合成的智能体框架，通过语义场景构建、闭环验证和感知压缩，实现高质量、可扩展的机器人操作数据集生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型需要大量语义连贯且物理可行的数据集，但传统场景生成方法缺乏上下文感知能力，导致生成的环境语义信息不足且目标位置不可达，造成任务过早失败。

Method: 1) 引入修复引导的场景构建，系统安排上下文感知的布局；2) 集成功能元数据与基于视觉语言模型的闭环验证机制，作为视觉批评家过滤错误；3) 实现感知驱动的压缩算法，减少90%以上文件大小。

Result: V-CAGE能够自动化端到端流程，实现多样化、高质量机器人操作数据集的高度可扩展合成，同时保持下游VLA训练效果不受影响。

Conclusion: 通过集中语义布局规划和视觉自验证，V-CAGE解决了现有数据合成方法的局限性，为大规模VLA模型训练提供了高效的数据生成解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models requires massive datasets that are both semantically coherent and physically feasible. However, existing scene generation methods often lack context-awareness, making it difficult to synthesize high-fidelity environments embedded with rich semantic information, frequently resulting in unreachable target positions that cause tasks to fail prematurely. We present V-CAGE (Vision-Closed-loop Agentic Generation Engine), an agentic framework for autonomous robotic data synthesis. Unlike traditional scripted pipelines, V-CAGE operates as an embodied agentic system, leveraging foundation models to bridge high-level semantic reasoning with low-level physical interaction. Specifically, we introduce Inpainting-Guided Scene Construction to systematically arrange context-aware layouts, ensuring that the generated scenes are both semantically structured and kinematically reachable. To ensure trajectory correctness, we integrate functional metadata with a Vision-Language Model based closed-loop verification mechanism, acting as a visual critic to rigorously filter out silent failures and sever the error propagation chain. Finally, to overcome the storage bottleneck of massive video datasets, we implement a perceptually-driven compression algorithm that achieves over 90\% filesize reduction without compromising downstream VLA training efficacy. By centralizing semantic layout planning and visual self-verification, V-CAGE automates the end-to-end pipeline, enabling the highly scalable synthesis of diverse, high-quality robotic manipulation datasets.

</details>


### [129] [Towards Lifelong Aerial Autonomy: Geometric Memory Management for Continual Visual Place Recognition in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09038)
*Xingyu Shao,Zhiqiang Yan,Liangzheng Sun,Mengfan He,Chao Chen,Jinhui Zhang,Chunyu Li,Ziyang Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于空中视觉地点识别的异构记忆框架，通过解耦地理知识为静态卫星锚点和动态经验回放缓冲区，解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中视觉地点识别在变化环境条件下需要鲁棒的定位能力，但现有持续学习方法在处理地理特征严重类内变化时容易失败，且受限于机载存储约束。

Method: 将空中VPR建模为基于任务的域增量学习问题，提出"学习与处置"管道，将地理知识解耦为静态卫星锚点（保留全局几何先验）和动态经验回放缓冲区（保留域特定特征），并引入空间约束分配策略优化缓冲区选择。

Result: 实验表明该架构显著提升空间泛化能力，多样性驱动的缓冲区选择比随机基线在知识保留上提升7.8%，最大化结构多样性在无序序列中实现更好的可塑性-稳定性平衡。

Conclusion: 在终身空中自主系统中，维持结构特征覆盖比样本难度更重要，能更有效地解决灾难性遗忘问题，为地理特征严重变化的持续学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Robust geo-localization in changing environmental conditions is critical for long-term aerial autonomy. While visual place recognition (VPR) models perform well when airborne views match the training domain, adapting them to shifting distributions during sequential missions triggers catastrophic forgetting. Existing continual learning (CL) methods often fail here because geographic features exhibit severe intra-class variations. In this work, we formulate aerial VPR as a mission-based domain-incremental learning (DIL) problem and propose a novel heterogeneous memory framework. To respect strict onboard storage constraints, our "Learn-and-Dispose" pipeline decouples geographic knowledge into static satellite anchors (preserving global geometric priors) and a dynamic experience replay buffer (retaining domain-specific features). We introduce a spatially-constrained allocation strategy that optimizes buffer selection based on sample difficulty or feature space diversity. To facilitate systematic assessment, we provide three evaluation criteria and a comprehensive benchmark derived from 21 diverse mission sequences. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our architecture significantly boosts spatial generalization; our diversity-driven buffer selection outperforms the random baseline by 7.8% in knowledge retention. Unlike class-mean preservation methods that fail in unstructured environments, maximizing structural diversity achieves a superior plasticity-stability balance and ensures order-agnostic robustness across randomized sequences. These results prove that maintaining structural feature coverage is more critical than sample difficulty for resolving catastrophic forgetting in lifelong aerial autonomy.

</details>


### [130] [{\sf TriDeliver}: Cooperative Air-Ground Instant Delivery with UAVs, Couriers, and Crowdsourced Ground Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09049)
*Junhui Gao,Yan Pan,Qianru Wang,Wenzhe Hou,Yiqin Deng,Liangliang Jiang,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TriDeliver提出首个分层协作框架，整合快递员、无人机和众包地面车辆进行即时配送，通过迁移学习从快递员历史行为中提取知识并转移给无人机和地面车辆，显著降低配送成本和时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即时配送需求激增，但现有配送方式各有局限：快递员效率低/人力短缺，无人机飞行控制受限，众包代理能力动态变化。单一方式无法满足需求，需要整合多种配送资源。

Method: 设计TriDeliver分层协作框架，整合快递员、无人机和众包地面车辆。采用迁移学习算法从快递员历史行为中提取配送知识，迁移到无人机和地面车辆并进行微调，用于高效包裹调度。

Result: 基于一个月真实轨迹和配送数据评估：1)相比最先进的无人机-快递员协作配送，成本降低65.8%；2)配送时间减少17.7%，成本降低9.8%，对众包车辆原任务影响减少43.6%。

Conclusion: TriDeliver通过整合多种配送资源和迁移学习技术，显著提升了即时配送效率，降低了成本，即使使用简单神经网络表示迁移知识也能取得显著改进。

Abstract: Instant delivery, shipping items before critical deadlines, is essential in daily life. While multiple delivery agents, such as couriers, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), and crowdsourced agents, have been widely employed, each of them faces inherent limitations (e.g., low efficiency/labor shortages, flight control, and dynamic capabilities, respectively), preventing them from meeting the surging demands alone. This paper proposes {\sf TriDeliver}, the first hierarchical cooperative framework, integrating human couriers, UAVs, and crowdsourced ground vehicles (GVs) for efficient instant delivery. To obtain the initial scheduling knowledge for GVs and UAVs as well as improve the cooperative delivery performance, we design a Transfer Learning (TL)-based algorithm to extract delivery knowledge from couriers' behavioral history and transfer their knowledge to UAVs and GVs with fine-tunings, which is then used to dispatch parcels for efficient delivery. Evaluated on one-month real-world trajectory and delivery datasets, it has been demonstrated that 1) by integrating couriers, UAVs, and crowdsourced GVs, {\sf TriDeliver} reduces the delivery cost by $65.8\%$ versus state-of-the-art cooperative delivery by UAVs and couriers; 2) {\sf TriDeliver} achieves further improvements in terms of delivery time ($-17.7\%$), delivery cost ($-9.8\%$), and impacts on original tasks of crowdsourced GVs ($-43.6\%$), even with the representation of the transferred knowledge by simple neural networks, respectively.

</details>


### [131] [On the Terminology and Geometric Aspects of Redundant Parallel Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09156)
*Andreas Mueller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并联机器人冗余分类的统一术语，引入构型空间模型，区分运动学冗余和驱动冗余，通过非线性控制系统分类不同驱动方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并联机器人(PKM)存在运动学冗余和驱动冗余，但文献中对驱动冗余的分类不一致，需要建立统一的术语和分类框架

Method: 引入以构型空间为核心的动力学模型，区分构型空间、输出和输入奇异性，建立非线性控制系统，定义驱动度(DOA)和自由度(DOF)的关系来分类驱动冗余

Result: 提出了并联机器人冗余分类的一致术语，阐明了构型空间几何的重要性，证明了冗余驱动方案可以避免输入奇异性，建立了基于驱动度的分类方法

Conclusion: 该研究为并联机器人的冗余分类提供了统一的理论框架，通过构型空间模型和驱动度概念，能够清晰地区分和分类不同的驱动冗余方案

Abstract: Parallel kinematics machines (PKM) can exhibit kinematic as well as actuation redundancy. While the meaning of kinematic redundancy has been clarified already for serial manipulators, actuation redundancy, that is only possible in PKM, is differently classified in the literature. In this paper a consistent terminology for general redundant PKM is proposed. A kinematic model is introduced with the configuration space (c-space) as central part. The notion of kinematic redundancy is recalled for PKM. C-space, output, and input singularities are distinguished. The significance of the c-space geometry is emphasized, and it is pointed out geometrically that input singularities can be avoided by redundant actuation schemes. In order to distinguish different actuation schemes of PKM a non-linear control system is introduced whose dynamics evolves on the c-space. The degree of actuation (DOA) is introduced as the number of independent control vector fields, and PKM are classified as full-actuated and underactuated. Relating this DOA to the degree of freedom (DOF) allows to classify the actuation redundancy.

</details>


### [132] [Soft Electroadhesive Feet for Micro Aerial Robots Perching on Smooth and Curved Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09270)
*Chen Liu,Sonu Feroz,Ketao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了用于微型飞行器栖息的软可拉伸电粘附垫，通过实验比较了法向和剪切粘附力，并在Crazyflie四旋翼上成功演示了在平面和曲面上的栖息能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电粘附技术能为微型飞行机器人提供可开关的粘附力，实现表面栖息，但目前软可拉伸电粘附垫的实际应用有限，需要开发更实用的制造方法和验证其栖息性能。

Method: 1) 开发了制造正弦波和同心圆电极软可拉伸电粘附垫的高效工作流程；2) 使用Instron测试装置在0kV和4.8kV条件下系统比较法向和剪切粘附力；3) 在Crazyflie四旋翼上安装电粘附脚，在平面和曲面基板上进行栖息演示。

Result: 剪切粘附力占主导，在部分垫接触情况下可达约3N；法向粘附力相对较小且强烈依赖基板特性。Crazyflie原型机能在光滑塑料表面（包括曲面）上重复附着，断电后能快速脱离。

Conclusion: 软电粘附脚在微型飞行器中具有轻量化和可靠栖息的潜力，剪切粘附力是实现有效栖息的关键因素，该技术适用于各种表面几何形状。

Abstract: Electroadhesion (EA) provides electrically switchable adhesion and is a promising mechanism for perching micro aerial robots on smooth surfaces. However, practical implementations of soft and stretchable EA pads for aerial perching remain limited. This work presents (i) an efficient workflow for fabricating soft, stretchable electroadhesive pads with sinusoidal wave and concentric-circle electrodes in multiple sizes, (ii) a controlled experimental comparison of normal and shear adhesion under inactive (0 kV) and active (4.8 kV) conditions using an Instron-based setup, and (iii) a perching demonstration using a Crazyflie quadrotor equipped with electroadhesive feet on flat and curved substrates. Experimental results show that shear adhesion dominates, reaching forces on the order of 3 N with partial pad contact, while normal adhesion is comparatively small and strongly dependent on substrate properties. The Crazyflie prototype demonstrates repeatable attachment on smooth plastic surfaces, including curved geometries, as well as rapid detachment when the voltage is removed. These results highlight the potential of soft electroadhesive feet for lightweight and reliable perching in micro aerial vehicles (MAVs).

</details>


### [133] [Characterizing Lidar Range-Measurement Ambiguity due to Multiple Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09282)
*Jason H. Rife,Yifan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 分析激光雷达在道路环境中可能产生概率性多回波的情况，并提供了两种机械旋转雷达的代表性累积分布函数，探讨其对定位算法的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光雷达在自动驾驶车辆定位中至关重要，但现有算法通常假设每条射线只返回一个表面，而实际中激光雷达在遇到多个散射表面时可能产生概率性测量，这种性能在道路环境中尚未被充分理解。

Method: 分析激光雷达数据集，识别具有概率性回波的射线路径，为两种不同机械旋转激光雷达单元提供代表性累积分布函数，并提出定性方法评估概率性多回波对激光雷达定位的影响。

Result: 展示了两种机械旋转激光雷达在特定射线路径上的概率性返回特征，提供了累积分布函数来描述这些现象。

Conclusion: 激光雷达在道路环境中确实存在概率性多回波现象，这会影响定位算法的准确性，需要开发更鲁棒的算法来处理这种不确定性。

Abstract: Reliable position and attitude sensing is critical for highly automated vehicles that operate on conventional roadways. Lidar sensors are increasingly incorporated into pose-estimation systems. Despite its great utility, lidar is a complex sensor, and its performance in roadway environments is not yet well understood. For instance, it is often assumed in lidar-localization algorithms that a lidar will always identify a unique surface along a given raypath. However, this assumption is not always true, as ample prior evidence exists to suggest that lidar units may generate measurements probabilistically when more than one scattering surface appears within the lidar's conical beam. In this paper, we analyze lidar datasets to characterize cases with probabilistic returns along particular raypaths. Our contribution is to present representative cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) for raypaths observed by two different mechanically rotating lidar units with stationary bases. In subsequent discussion, we outline a qualitative methodology to assess the effect of probabilistic multi-return cases on lidar-based localization.

</details>


### [134] [A Benchmark of Dexterity for Anthropomorphic Robotic Hands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09294)
*Davide Liconti,Yuning Zhou,Yasunori Toshimitsu,Ronan Hinchet,Robert K. Katzschmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POMDAR是一个基于任务性能的机器人灵巧性基准测试，通过结构化操作任务和量化评分来统一评估仿人机器人手的设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人灵巧性定义模糊且评估标准不一致，导致不同设计难以进行有意义的比较，需要基于可测量结果的统一性能定义。

Method: 从人类运动控制分类学系统推导出结构化操作任务集，包含垂直/水平配置、连续旋转和纯抓取四种配置，通过机械支架约束任务运动并抑制补偿策略，定义结合任务正确性和执行速度的量化评分指标。

Result: 开发了POMDAR基准测试，在真实世界和仿真中实现，提供开源标准化评估框架，能够客观、可重复、可解释地评估不同手部设计。

Conclusion: POMDAR为仿人机器人手提供了基于分类学的标准化基准测试，促进灵巧操作平台的系统化发展，实现一致的设计比较和评估。

Abstract: Dexterity is a central yet ambiguously defined concept in the design and evaluation of anthropomorphic robotic hands. In practice, the term is often used inconsistently, with different systems evaluated under disparate criteria, making meaningful comparisons across designs difficult. This highlights the need for a unified, performance-based definition of dexterity grounded in measurable outcomes rather than proxy metrics. In this work, we introduce POMDAR, a comprehensive dexterity benchmark that formalizes dexterity as task performance across a structured set of manipulation and grasping motions. The benchmark was systematically derived from established taxonomies in human motor control. It is implemented in both real-world and simulation and includes four manipulation configurations: vertical and horizontal configurations, continuous rotation, and pure grasping. The task designs contain mechanical scaffolding to constrain task motion, suppress compensatory strategies, and enable metrics to be measured unambiguously. We define a quantitative scoring metric combining task correctness and execution speed, effectively measuring dexterity as throughput. This enables objective, reproducible, and interpretable evaluation across different hand designs. POMDAR provides an open-source, standardized, and taxonomy-grounded benchmark for consistent comparison and evaluation of anthropomorphic robot hands to facilitate a systematic advancement of dexterous manipulation platforms. CAD, simulation files, and evaluation videos are publicly available at https://srl-ethz.github.io/POMDAR/.

</details>


### [135] [Online Intention Prediction via Control-Informed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09303)
*Tianyu Zhou,Zihao Liang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线意图预测框架，实时估计自主系统的目标状态，即使意图随时间变化或系统包含未知参数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统在复杂环境中需要实时预测时变意图，但现有方法难以处理动态变化的目标和未知参数

Method: 将问题建模为逆最优控制/逆强化学习任务，采用移动时域策略折扣过时信息，结合在线控制感知学习实现高效梯度计算和参数更新

Result: 在不同噪声水平的仿真和四旋翼无人机硬件实验中，该方法在复杂环境中实现了准确、自适应的意图预测

Conclusion: 提出的在线意图预测框架能有效处理时变意图和未知参数，为自主系统在动态环境中的实时决策提供了可靠解决方案

Abstract: This paper presents an online intention prediction framework for estimating the goal state of autonomous systems in real time, even when intention is time-varying, and system dynamics or objectives include unknown parameters. The problem is formulated as an inverse optimal control / inverse reinforcement learning task, with the intention treated as a parameter in the objective. A shifting horizon strategy discounts outdated information, while online control-informed learning enables efficient gradient computation and online parameter updates. Simulations under varying noise levels and hardware experiments on a quadrotor drone demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves accurate, adaptive intention prediction in complex environments.

</details>


### [136] [Robust Adaptive Backstepping Impedance Control of Robots in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09323)
*Reza Nazmara,Alap Kshirsagar,Jan Peters,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种鲁棒自适应反步阻抗控制(RABIC)策略，用于处理接触丰富和不确定环境中的机器人控制问题，通过自适应阻抗控制和不确定性估计实现安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人经常在接触丰富和不确定的环境中操作，需要处理外部干扰、未建模动态等不确定性，同时实现精确的轨迹跟踪和力控制。

Method: 采用反步自适应阻抗控制作为内环跟踪参考阻抗模型，使用泰勒级数估计器估计系统动态，自适应估计器确定外力的上界，确保系统半全局实际有限时间稳定。

Result: 通过移动机械臂仿真和Franka Emika Panda机器人实验验证，相比PD控制表现出更安全的性能，同时确保轨迹跟踪和力监测。

Conclusion: RABIC框架为移动和固定串联机械臂的自适应和学习型阻抗控制提供了坚实基础，是未来研究的良好起点。

Abstract: This paper presents a Robust Adaptive Backstepping Impedance Control (RABIC) strategy for robots operating in contact-rich and uncertain environments. The proposed control strategy considers the complete coupled dynamics of the system and explicitly accounts for key sources of uncertainty, including external disturbances and unmodeled dynamics, while not requiring the robot's dynamic parameters in implementation. We propose a backstepping-based adaptive impedance control scheme for the inner loop to track the reference impedance model. To handle uncertainties, we employ a Taylor series-based estimator for system dynamics and an adaptive estimator for determining the upper bound of external forces. Stability analysis demonstrates the semi-global practical finite-time stability of the overall system. To demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, a simulated mobile manipulator scenario and experimental evaluations on a real Franka Emika Panda robot were conducted. The proposed approach exhibits safer performance compared to PD control while ensuring trajectory tracking and force monitoring. Overall, the RABIC framework provides a solid basis for future research on adaptive and learning-based impedance control for coupled mobile and fixed serially linked manipulators.

</details>


### [137] [Multimodal Anomaly Detection for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09326)
*Guilherme Ribeiro,Iordanis Antypas,Leonardo Bizzaro,João Bimbo,Nuno Cruz Garcia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MADRI框架通过将视频流转换为语义特征向量，结合机器人内部传感器数据和场景图，进行重建式异常检测，提升人机协作中的异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中，及时检测可能导致系统故障或不安全行为的意外事件至关重要。虽然重建模型在HRI中已被广泛探索，但直接在特征向量上操作的方法仍未被充分研究。

Method: 提出MADRI框架：1) 将视频流转换为语义特征向量；2) 结合机器人内部传感器读数；3) 加入场景图；4) 基于这些多模态特征进行重建式异常检测。

Result: 实验结果表明：仅基于视觉特征向量的重建已能有效检测异常，而结合其他模态进一步提升了检测性能，验证了多模态特征重建在人机协作异常检测中的优势。

Conclusion: 多模态特征重建方法为人类-机器人协作提供了鲁棒的异常检测框架，能够同时捕捉视觉环境中的外部异常和机器人内部的故障。

Abstract: Ensuring safety and reliability in human-robot interaction (HRI) requires the timely detection of unexpected events that could lead to system failures or unsafe behaviours. Anomaly detection thus plays a critical role in enabling robots to recognize and respond to deviations from normal operation during collaborative tasks. While reconstruction models have been actively explored in HRI, approaches that operate directly on feature vectors remain largely unexplored. In this work, we propose MADRI, a framework that first transforms video streams into semantically meaningful feature vectors before performing reconstruction-based anomaly detection. Additionally, we augment these visual feature vectors with the robot's internal sensors' readings and a Scene Graph, enabling the model to capture both external anomalies in the visual environment and internal failures within the robot itself. To evaluate our approach, we collected a custom dataset consisting of a simple pick-and-place robotic task under normal and anomalous conditions. Experimental results demonstrate that reconstruction on vision-based feature vectors alone is effective for detecting anomalies, while incorporating other modalities further improves detection performance, highlighting the benefits of multimodal feature reconstruction for robust anomaly detection in human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [138] [VAG: Dual-Stream Video-Action Generation for Embodied Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09330)
*Xiaolei Lang,Yang Wang,Yukun Zhou,Chaojun Ni,Kerui Li,Jiagang Zhu,Tianze Liu,Jiajun Lv,Xingxing Zuo,Yun Ye,Guan Huang,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAG是一个统一的流匹配双流框架，能够联合生成视频和动作，解决了现有世界模型缺乏配对动作轨迹的问题，为机器人数据合成提供了实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础模型需要大量人类遥操作数据，但收集任务特定演示成本高昂。现有世界模型不提供配对动作轨迹，而世界-动作模型视频-动作对齐性差，两阶段流水线效率低且误差累积。

Method: 提出VAG框架：基于流匹配的双流架构，在视觉和语言条件下联合生成视频和动作；通过同步两个分支的去噪过程，并使用自适应3D池化机制将紧凑的全局视频上下文传递到动作分支。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界环境中，VAG能生成对齐的视频-动作对，具有竞争力的预测质量；支持可执行轨迹回放，提供有用的合成预训练数据，改善下游策略泛化能力。

Conclusion: VAG作为实用的世界-动作模型，在具身数据合成方面具有潜力，通过联合生成对齐的视频和动作，解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot foundation models trained on large-scale human teleoperation data have enabled robots to perform increasingly complex real-world tasks. However, scaling these systems remains difficult because collecting task-specific demonstrations is expensive and labor-intensive. Synthetic data, especially generated videos, offer a promising direction, but existing World Models (WMs) are not directly suitable for policy learning since they do not provide paired action trajectories. World-Action (WA) models partially address this by predicting actions with visual outputs, yet often lack strong video-action alignment, while two-stage pipelines that generate video first and then infer actions introduce inefficiency and error accumulation. To address these limitations, we propose VAG, a unified flow-matching-based dual-stream framework that jointly generates video and action under visual and language conditioning. By synchronizing denoising in both branches and using an adaptive 3D pooling mechanism to transfer compact global video context to the action branch, VAG improves cross-modal consistency during generation. Across both simulated and real-world settings, VAG produces aligned video-action pairs with competitive prediction quality, supports executable trajectory replay, and provides useful synthetic pretraining data that improves downstream policy generalization, indicating its potential as a practical world-action model for embodied data synthesis.

</details>


### [139] [Musculoskeletal Motion Imitation for Learning Personalized Exoskeleton Control Policy in Impaired Gait](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09431)
*Itak Choi,Ilseung Park,Eni Halilaj,Inseung Kang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个结合肌肉骨骼模拟与强化学习的设备无关框架，用于为下肢外骨骼生成可扩展的个性化辅助控制策略，无需大量数据收集或物理试验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前下肢外骨骼通用控制策略的设计受到大量数据收集或迭代优化程序的限制，这阻碍了临床人群的可及性。需要一种可扩展的方法来为健全和临床人群提供个性化辅助。

Method: 开发了一个设备无关框架，将生理上合理的肌肉骨骼模拟与强化学习相结合。该框架生成的控制策略不仅能产生生理上合理的运动动力学，还能捕捉临床观察到的补偿策略，为健康和病理步态提供统一计算模型。

Result: 控制策略生成的髋关节和踝关节辅助扭矩曲线与人类实验中验证的最先进曲线一致，并在不同步行速度下持续降低代谢成本。对于模拟的受损步态模型，学习到的控制策略产生不对称、缺陷特异性的外骨骼辅助，改善了能量效率和双侧运动对称性。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习的生理合理肌肉骨骼模拟可以作为跨健全和临床人群的个性化外骨骼控制的可扩展基础，消除了大量物理试验的需求。

Abstract: Designing generalizable control policies for lower-limb exoskeletons remains fundamentally constrained by exhaustive data collection or iterative optimization procedures, which limit accessibility to clinical populations. To address this challenge, we introduce a device-agnostic framework that combines physiologically plausible musculoskeletal simulation with reinforcement learning to enable scalable personalized exoskeleton assistance for both able-bodied and clinical populations. Our control policies not only generate physiologically plausible locomotion dynamics but also capture clinically observed compensatory strategies under targeted muscular deficits, providing a unified computational model of both healthy and pathological gait. Without task-specific tuning, the resulting exoskeleton control policies produce assistive torque profiles at the hip and ankle that align with state-of-the-art profiles validated in human experiments, while consistently reducing metabolic cost across walking speeds. For simulated impaired-gait models, the learned control policies yield asymmetric, deficit-specific exoskeleton assistance that improves both energetic efficiency and bilateral kinematic symmetry without explicit prescription of the target gait pattern. These results demonstrate that physiologically plausible musculoskeletal simulation via reinforcement learning can serve as a scalable foundation for personalized exoskeleton control across both able-bodied and clinical populations, eliminating the need for extensive physical trials.

</details>


### [140] [Adaptor: Advancing Assistive Teleoperation with Few-Shot Learning and Cross-Operator Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09462)
*Yu Liu,Yihang Yin,Tianlv Huang,Fei Yan,Yuan Xu,Weinan Hong,Wei Han,Yue Cao,Xiangyu Chen,Zipei Fan,Xuan Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Adaptor是一个少样本框架，通过两阶段处理（预处理和政策学习）解决辅助遥操作中因操作者习惯差异导致的意图识别不稳定问题，实现跨操作者的鲁棒意图识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辅助遥操作通过共享控制提高效率，但不同操作者的习惯和专业知识差异导致高度异构的轨迹分布，这削弱了意图识别的稳定性。

Method: Adaptor采用两阶段方法：1) 预处理阶段通过噪声注入合成轨迹扰动来建模意图不确定性，并进行几何感知的关键帧提取；2) 政策学习阶段使用意图专家编码处理后的轨迹，并将其与预训练视觉语言模型上下文融合，以条件化动作专家生成动作。

Result: 在真实世界和模拟基准测试中，Adaptor实现了最先进的性能，提高了成功率和效率。此外，该方法在不同专业水平的操作者间表现出低方差，展示了鲁棒的跨操作者泛化能力。

Conclusion: Adaptor框架能够有效解决辅助遥操作中的跨操作者意图识别问题，通过建模意图不确定性和融合多模态信息，实现了稳定且高效的意图识别系统。

Abstract: Assistive teleoperation enhances efficiency via shared control, yet inter-operator variability, stemming from diverse habits and expertise, induces highly heterogeneous trajectory distributions that undermine intent recognition stability. We present Adaptor, a few-shot framework for robust cross-operator intent recognition. The Adaptor bridges the domain gap through two stages: (i) preprocessing, which models intent uncertainty by synthesizing trajectory perturbations via noise injection and performs geometry-aware keyframe extraction; and (ii) policy learning, which encodes the processed trajectories with an Intention Expert and fuses them with the pre-trained vision-language model context to condition an Action Expert for action generation. Experiments on real-world and simulated benchmarks demonstrate that Adaptor achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving success rates and efficiency over baselines. Moreover, the method exhibits low variance across operators with varying expertise, demonstrating robust cross-operator generalization.

</details>


### [141] [SafeMind: A Risk-Aware Differentiable Control Framework for Adaptive and Safe Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09474)
*Zukun Zhang,Kai Shu,Mingqiao Mo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SafeMind：一种可微分的随机安全控制框架，将概率控制屏障函数与语义上下文理解和元自适应风险校准相结合，为四足机器人提供形式化安全保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的四足控制器虽然实现了令人印象深刻的敏捷性，但通常在模型不确定性、感知噪声和非结构化接触条件下缺乏形式化的安全保证

Method: 通过可微分的随机安全控制框架，将概率控制屏障函数与语义上下文理解和元自适应风险校准统一起来。使用方差感知的屏障约束嵌入可微分二次规划，保持端到端训练的梯度流。语义到约束编码器使用感知或语言线索调节安全边界，元自适应学习器在不同环境中持续调整风险敏感性

Result: 在Unitree A1和ANYmal C上以200Hz部署，在12种地形类型、动态障碍物、形态扰动和语义定义任务中验证。相比最先进的CBF、MPC和混合RL基线，安全违规减少3-10倍，能耗降低10-15%，同时保持实时控制性能

Conclusion: SafeMind为四足机器人在不确定环境中的安全控制提供了理论保证和实际验证的有效解决方案，显著提高了安全性和能效

Abstract: Learning-based quadruped controllers achieve impressive agility but typically lack formal safety guarantees under model uncertainty, perception noise, and unstructured contact conditions. We introduce SafeMind, a differentiable stochastic safety-control framework that unifies probabilistic Control Barrier Functions with semantic context understanding and meta-adaptive risk calibration. SafeMind explicitly models epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty through a variance-aware barrier constraint embedded in a differentiable quadratic program, thereby preserving gradient flow for end-to-end training. A semantics-to-constraint encoder modulates safety margins using perceptual or language cues, while a meta-adaptive learner continuously adjusts risk sensitivity across environments. We provide theoretical conditions for probabilistic forward invariance, feasibility, and stability under stochastic dynamics. SafeMind is deployed on Unitree A1 and ANYmal C at 200~Hz and validated across 12 terrain types, dynamic obstacles, morphology perturbations, and semantically defined tasks. Experiments show that SafeMind reduces safety violations by 3--10x and energy consumption by 10--15% relative to state-of-the-art CBF, MPC, and hybrid RL baselines, while maintaining real-time control performance.

</details>


### [142] [Sim-to-Real Transfer for Muscle-Actuated Robots via Generalized Actuator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09487)
*Jan Schneider,Mridul Mahajan,Le Chen,Simon Guist,Bernhard Schölkopf,Ingmar Posner,Dieter Büchler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GeAN方法，通过神经网络建模肌腱驱动软体肌肉驱动器的复杂非线性特性，实现首个四自由度肌肉驱动机器人臂的仿真到现实策略迁移


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动配合软体肌肉驱动能使机器人更快更安全并加速技能学习，但由于固有的非线性、摩擦和迟滞特性，建模和控制困难，阻碍了从仿真到现实系统的策略迁移

Method: 提出广义执行器网络(GeAN)，学习复杂驱动系统的神经网络模型，结合刚体动力学仿真，仅需关节位置轨迹而不需要扭矩传感器，实现跨多种机器人的驱动模型识别

Result: 在PAMY2肌腱驱动气动人工肌肉机器人上成功部署了精确目标到达和动态球入杯策略，实现了首个四自由度肌肉驱动机器人臂的仿真到现实迁移

Conclusion: GeAN方法有效解决了肌腱驱动软体肌肉系统的建模和控制挑战，为这类复杂驱动系统的仿真到现实迁移提供了可行方案

Abstract: Tendon drives paired with soft muscle actuation enable faster and safer robots while potentially accelerating skill acquisition. Still, these systems are rarely used in practice due to inherent nonlinearities, friction, and hysteresis, which complicate modeling and control. So far, these challenges have hindered policy transfer from simulation to real systems. To bridge this gap, we propose a sim-to-real pipeline that learns a neural network model of this complex actuation and leverages established rigid body simulation for the arm dynamics and interactions with the environment. Our method, called Generalized Actuator Network (GeAN), enables actuation model identification across a wide range of robots by learning directly from joint position trajectories rather than requiring torque sensors. Using GeAN on PAMY2, a tendon-driven robot powered by pneumatic artificial muscles, we successfully deploy precise goal-reaching and dynamic ball-in-a-cup policies trained entirely in simulation. To the best of our knowledge, this result constitutes the first successful sim-to-real transfer for a four-degrees-of-freedom muscle-actuated robot arm.

</details>


### [143] [Physics-Informed Reinforcement Learning of Spatial Density Velocity Potentials for Map-Free Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09499)
*Shathushan Sivashangaran,Apoorva Khairnar,Sepideh Gohari,Vihaan Dutta,Azim Eskandarian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度强化学习的自动驾驶赛车方法，通过深度测量频谱分布参数化非线性车辆动力学，使用物理信息奖励实现端到端控制，在未知赛道上比人类演示快12%，计算量仅为传统方法的1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶赛车方法存在局限性：行为克隆受限于人类反应时间，深度强化学习需要大量碰撞训练且难以从仿真迁移到现实。需要一种能在未知赛道上实现时间最优控制和超车，同时保持计算效率和硬件稳定性的方法。

Method: 使用深度强化学习，通过深度测量频谱分布参数化非线性车辆动力学；采用非几何、物理信息奖励函数；用人工神经网络推断车辆时间最优和超车控制；结合物理引擎利用感知奖励和值函数视野截断替代显式碰撞惩罚。

Result: 在按比例缩放的硬件上，该方法在未知赛道上比人类演示快12%；计算量仅为行为克隆和基于模型的深度强化学习的1%；成功消除了从仿真到现实的迁移问题和方差导致的保守主义；能够最大化摩擦圆，轮胎动力学接近经验Pacejka模型。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了高效的端到端自动驾驶赛车控制，在未知赛道上超越了人类性能，同时保持了计算效率和硬件稳定性。系统识别揭示了网络的功能分叉：第一层压缩空间观测提取数字化赛道特征，第二层编码非线性动力学。

Abstract: Autonomous racing without prebuilt maps is a grand challenge for embedded robotics that requires kinodynamic planning from instantaneous sensor data at the acceleration and tire friction limits. Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) generalization to various racetrack configurations utilizes Machine Learning (ML) to encode the mathematical relation between sensor data and vehicle actuation for end-to-end control, with implicit localization. These comprise Behavioral Cloning (BC) that is capped to human reaction times and Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) which requires large-scale collisions for comprehensive training that can be infeasible without simulation but is arduous to transfer to reality, thus exhibiting greater performance than BC in simulation, but actuation instability on hardware. This paper presents a DRL method that parameterizes nonlinear vehicle dynamics from the spectral distribution of depth measurements with a non-geometric, physics-informed reward, to infer vehicle time-optimal and overtaking racing controls with an Artificial Neural Network (ANN) that utilizes less than 1% of the computation of BC and model-based DRL. Slaloming from simulation to reality transfer and variance-induced conservatism are eliminated with the combination of a physics engine exploit-aware reward and the replacement of an explicit collision penalty with an implicit truncation of the value horizon. The policy outperforms human demonstrations by 12% in OOD tracks on proportionally scaled hardware, by maximizing the friction circle with tire dynamics that resemble an empirical Pacejka tire model. System identification illuminates a functional bifurcation where the first layer compresses spatial observations to extract digitized track features with higher resolution in corner apexes, and the second encodes nonlinear dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [OpenKedge: Governing Agentic Mutation with Execution-Bound Safety and Evidence Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08601)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenKedge是一个协议，将API调用从即时状态突变重新定义为受治理的过程，通过声明式意图提案、确定性状态评估和执行合同来确保AI代理操作的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于API的自主AI代理架构存在根本缺陷：概率性系统在没有足够上下文、协调或安全保障的情况下直接执行状态突变，导致安全风险。

Method: OpenKedge协议要求参与者提交声明式意图提案，这些提案在确定性系统状态、时间信号和策略约束下评估。批准的意图被编译为执行合同，通过临时任务身份强制执行，并建立意图到执行的证据链（IEEC）提供可验证的审计追踪。

Result: OpenKedge在多代理冲突场景和云基础设施突变中能够确定性仲裁竞争意图，限制不安全执行，同时保持高吞吐量，为规模化代理系统提供了安全基础。

Conclusion: OpenKedge通过将突变重新定义为受治理的过程，从被动过滤转向预防性执行边界强制执行，为自主AI代理提供了可验证、可重构的安全操作框架。

Abstract: The rise of autonomous AI agents exposes a fundamental flaw in API-centric architectures: probabilistic systems directly execute state mutations without sufficient context, coordination, or safety guarantees. We introduce OpenKedge, a protocol that redefines mutation as a governed process rather than an immediate consequence of API invocation. OpenKedge requires actors to submit declarative intent proposals, which are evaluated against deterministically derived system state, temporal signals, and policy constraints prior to execution. Approved intents are compiled into execution contracts that strictly bound permitted actions, resource scope, and time, and are enforced via ephemeral, task-oriented identities. This shifts safety from reactive filtering to preventative, execution-bound enforcement. Crucially, OpenKedge introduces an Intent-to-Execution Evidence Chain (IEEC), which cryptographically links intent, context, policy decisions, execution bounds, and outcomes into a unified lineage. This transforms mutation into a verifiable and reconstructable process, enabling deterministic auditability and reasoning about system behavior. We evaluate OpenKedge across multi-agent conflict scenarios and cloud infrastructure mutations. Results show that the protocol deterministically arbitrates competing intents and cages unsafe execution while maintaining high throughput, establishing a principled foundation for safely operating agentic systems at scale.

</details>


### [145] [From Business Events to Auditable Decisions: Ontology-Governed Graph Simulation for Enterprise AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08603)
*Hongyin Zhu,Jinming Liang,Mengjun Hou,Ruifan Tang,Xianbin Zhu,Jingyuan Yang,Yuanman Mao,Feng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LOM-action 是一个企业AI系统，通过事件驱动的本体模拟来确保决策的可追溯性和可靠性，相比传统LLM代理系统有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的代理系统存在架构缺陷：它们从无限制的知识空间中回答问题，而没有首先模拟活跃业务场景如何为当前事件重塑该空间，导致决策流畅但缺乏依据且无法审计。

Method: LOM-action采用事件驱动的本体模拟：业务事件触发企业本体中编码的场景条件，驱动确定性图突变在隔离沙箱中，将工作子图副本演化为场景有效的模拟图G_sim；所有决策都从这个演化图中推导。核心流程是"事件→模拟→决策"，通过双模式架构（技能模式和推理模式）实现。

Result: LOM-action达到93.82%的准确率和98.74%的工具链F1分数，而前沿基线模型Doubao-1.8和DeepSeek-V3.2仅达到24-36%的F1分数，尽管有80%的准确率，暴露了"虚假准确率"现象。

Conclusion: 四倍的F1优势证实，本体治理、事件驱动的模拟，而非模型规模，是可信企业决策智能的架构前提。每个决策都产生完全可追溯的审计日志。

Abstract: Existing LLM-based agent systems share a common architectural failure: they answer from the unrestricted knowledge space without first simulating how active business scenarios reshape that space for the event at hand -- producing decisions that are fluent but ungrounded and carrying no audit trail. We present LOM-action, which equips enterprise AI with \emph{event-driven ontology simulation}: business events trigger scenario conditions encoded in the enterprise ontology~(EO), which drive deterministic graph mutations in an isolated sandbox, evolving a working copy of the subgraph into the scenario-valid simulation graph $G_{\text{sim}}$; all decisions are derived exclusively from this evolved graph. The core pipeline is \emph{event $\to$ simulation $\to$ decision}, realized through a dual-mode architecture -- \emph{skill mode} and \emph{reasoning mode}. Every decision produces a fully traceable audit log. LOM-action achieves 93.82% accuracy and 98.74% tool-chain F1 against frontier baselines Doubao-1.8 and DeepSeek-V3.2, which reach only 24--36% F1 despite 80% accuracy -- exposing the \emph{illusive accuracy} phenomenon. The four-fold F1 advantage confirms that ontology-governed, event-driven simulation, not model scale, is the architectural prerequisite for trustworthy enterprise decision intelligence.

</details>


### [146] [Sustained Impact of Agentic Personalisation in Marketing: A Longitudinal Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08621)
*Olivier Jeunen,Eleanor Hanna,Schaun Wheeler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究比较了人工管理与自主代理在CRM营销中的表现，发现人工管理能产生最高相对提升，而自主代理能持续维持正向提升，建议采用人机协同的共生模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CRM依赖基于规则的静态策略手动优化，虽然自适应学习系统承诺可扩展的个性化，但尚不清楚需要多少"人在回路"监督来维持长期性能提升。

Method: 进行了为期11个月的纵向案例研究，分析真实消费者应用中代理基础设施个性化营销信息。比较了两个阶段：主动阶段（营销人员直接策划内容、受众和策略）和被动阶段（代理从固定组件库自主操作）。

Result: 主动人工管理产生最高的相对参与度提升，但自主代理在被动阶段成功维持了正向提升。自主系统能够持续保持性能增益。

Conclusion: 建议采用共生模型：人工干预驱动战略初始化和发现，而自主代理确保性能增益的可扩展保留和维持。

Abstract: In consumer applications, Customer Relationship Management (CRM) has traditionally relied on the manual optimisation of static, rule-based messaging strategies. While adaptive and autonomous learning systems offer the promise of scalable personalisation, it remains unclear to what extent ``human-in-the-loop'' oversight is required to sustain performance uplift over time. This paper presents a longitudinal case study analysing a real-world consumer application that leverages agentic infrastructure to personalise marketing messaging for a large-scale user base over an 11-month period.
  We compare two distinct periods: an active phase where marketers directly curated content, audiences, and strategies -- followed immediately by a passive phase where agents operated autonomously from a fixed library of components. Our results demonstrate that whilst active human management generates the highest relative lift in engagement metrics, the autonomous agents successfully sustained a positive lift during the passive period. These findings suggest a symbiotic model where human intervention drives strategic initialisation and discovery, yet autonomous agents can ensure the scalable retention and preservation of performance gains.

</details>


### [147] [RAMP: Hybrid DRL for Online Learning of Numeric Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08685)
*Yarin Benyamin,Argaman Mordoch,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Roni Stern*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAMP提出了一种在线学习数值规划动作模型的策略，通过强化学习、动作模型学习和规划三者的协同，在交互中学习并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统规划算法需要预先定义动作模型，但获取这样的模型很困难。现有的数值领域动作模型学习方法都是离线的，需要专家轨迹作为输入，无法在线学习。

Method: RAMP策略同时训练深度强化学习策略、从历史交互中学习数值动作模型，并在可能时使用该模型规划未来动作。这三者形成正反馈循环：RL策略收集数据来优化动作模型，而规划器生成计划来继续训练RL策略。还开发了Numeric PDDLGym框架，将数值规划问题转换为Gym环境。

Result: 在标准IPC数值领域的实验结果表明，RAMP在可解性和规划质量方面显著优于著名的DRL算法PPO。

Conclusion: RAMP成功实现了数值规划动作模型的在线学习，通过RL、动作模型学习和规划的集成，形成了有效的正反馈循环，显著提升了性能。

Abstract: Automated planning algorithms require an action model specifying the preconditions and effects of each action, but obtaining such a model is often hard. Learning action models from observations is feasible, but existing algorithms for numeric domains are offline, requiring expert traces as input. We propose the Reinforcement learning, Action Model learning, and Planning (RAMP) strategy for learning numeric planning action models online via interactions with the environment. RAMP simultaneously trains a Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) policy, learns a numeric action model from past interactions, and uses that model to plan future actions when possible. These components form a positive feedback loop: the RL policy gathers data to refine the action model, while the planner generates plans to continue training the RL policy. To facilitate this integration of RL and numeric planning, we developed Numeric PDDLGym, an automated framework for converting numeric planning problems to Gym environments. Experimental results on standard IPC numeric domains show that RAMP significantly outperforms PPO, a well-known DRL algorithm, in terms of solvability and plan quality.

</details>


### [148] [Parameterized Complexity Of Representing Models Of MSO Formulas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08707)
*Petr Kučera,Petr Martinek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文扩展了Courcelle定理，证明MSO2公式的模型可以用决策图表示，其大小与树宽和公式大小呈参数化线性关系，并建立了与知识表示领域的连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Courcelle定理在参数化复杂度中很重要，但主要关注MSO2公式的可满足性检查。本研究旨在扩展该定理，探索MSO2公式模型（包含自由变量）的紧凑表示问题，连接Courcelle定理与知识表示领域。

Method: 1. 证明MSO2公式模型可以用决策图表示，其大小相对于树宽和公式大小呈参数化线性
2. 对于树宽，使用句子决策图(SDD)并获得参数化线性上界
3. 对于路径宽，使用有序二元决策图(OBDD)并获得参数化线性上界
4. 基于Razgon(2014)的OBDD大小下界，构造反例证明存在有界树宽图类不支持参数化大小的OBDD

Result: 1. 成功扩展Courcelle定理，证明MSO2公式模型可以用决策图紧凑表示
2. 获得SDD大小的参数化线性上界（针对树宽）
3. 获得OBDD大小的参数化线性上界（针对路径宽）
4. 证明存在MSO2公式和有界树宽图类，其OBDD表示无法实现参数化线性大小

Conclusion: 该研究为Courcelle定理提供了新的视角，将参数化复杂度与知识表示领域连接起来，展示了MSO2公式模型可以用决策图紧凑表示，但同时也揭示了OBDD表示在某些情况下的局限性。

Abstract: Monadic second order logic (MSO2) plays an important role in parameterized complexity due to the Courcelle's theorem. This theorem states that the problem of checking if a given graph has a property specified by a given MSO2 formula can be solved by a parameterized linear time algorithm with respect to the treewidth of the graph and the size of the formula. We extend this result by showing that models of MSO2 formula with free variables can be represented with a decision diagram whose size is parameterized linear in the above mentioned parameter. In particular, we show a parameterized linear upper bound on the size of a sentential decision diagram (SDD) when treewidth is considered and a parameterized linear upper bound on the size of an ordered binary decision diagram (OBDD) when considering the pathwidth in the parameter. In addition, building on a lower bound on the size of OBDD by Razgon (2014), we show that there is an MSO2 formula and a class of graphs with bounded treewidth which do not admit an OBDD with the size parameterized by the treewidth. Our result offers a new perspective on the Courcelle's theorem and connects it to the area of knowledge representation.

</details>


### [149] [Model Space Reasoning as Search in Feedback Space for Planning Domain Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08712)
*James Oswald,Daniel Oblinsky,Volodymyr Varha,Vasilije Dragovic,Harsha Kokel,Kavitha Srinivas,Michael Katz,Shirin Sohrabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究使用代理语言模型反馈框架从自然语言描述生成规划领域，通过符号反馈（如地标和VAL验证器输出）和启发式搜索优化领域质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型和推理模型有所发展，但从自然语言描述生成规划领域仍然是一个未解决的问题。现有LLM虽然能辅助领域生成，但生成高质量、可实际部署的领域仍有差距

Method: 采用代理语言模型反馈框架，在自然语言描述基础上添加少量符号信息，使用多种符号反馈机制（包括地标和VAL规划验证器输出），通过模型空间的启发式搜索来优化领域质量

Result: 论文评估了不同符号反馈形式下生成领域的质量，但摘要中未提供具体实验结果数据

Conclusion: 通过代理语言模型反馈框架结合符号反馈和启发式搜索，有望改进从自然语言生成规划领域的质量，但具体效果需要进一步实验验证

Abstract: The generation of planning domains from natural language descriptions remains an open problem even with the advent of large language models and reasoning models. Recent work suggests that while LLMs have the ability to assist with domain generation, they are still far from producing high quality domains that can be deployed in practice. To this end, we investigate the ability of an agentic language model feedback framework to generate planning domains from natural language descriptions that have been augmented with a minimal amount of symbolic information. In particular, we evaluate the quality of the generated domains under various forms of symbolic feedback, including landmarks, and output from the VAL plan validator. Using these feedback mechanisms, we experiment using heuristic search over model space to optimize domain quality.

</details>


### [150] [Artifacts as Memory Beyond the Agent Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08756)
*John D. Martin,Fraser Mince,Esra'a Saleh,Amy Pajak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出强化学习中环境可作为智能体外存的理论框架，证明某些观测（称为"人工制品"）可减少表示历史所需的信息量，并通过实验验证观察空间路径可降低学习高性能策略所需的内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情境认知理论认为智能行为不仅依赖内部记忆，还依赖智能体对环境资源的主动利用。本文旨在将这一直觉形式化到强化学习框架中，探索环境如何功能性地替代智能体的内部记忆。

Method: 引入数学框架形式化环境作为智能体外存的概念，定义"人工制品"（artifacts）作为能减少历史表示信息量的观测。通过实验验证当智能体观察空间路径时，学习高性能策略所需的内存会减少。

Result: 理论证明某些观测（人工制品）确实能减少表示历史所需的信息量。实验表明观察空间路径可降低学习高性能策略所需的内存，且这种效应是无意中通过智能体的感知流隐式产生的。

Conclusion: 研究为环境作为外存提供了形式化基础，满足先前用于支撑外部记忆账户的定性特性。未来工作可探索利用环境替代显式内部记忆的原则性方法，对强化学习和认知科学有重要启示。

Abstract: The situated view of cognition holds that intelligent behavior depends not only on internal memory, but on an agent's active use of environmental resources. Here, we begin formalizing this intuition within Reinforcement Learning (RL). We introduce a mathematical framing for how the environment can functionally serve as an agent's memory, and prove that certain observations, which we call artifacts, can reduce the information needed to represent history. We corroborate our theory with experiments showing that when agents observe spatial paths, the amount of memory required to learn a performant policy is reduced. Interestingly, this effect arises unintentionally, and implicitly through the agent's sensory stream. We discuss the implications of our findings, and show they satisfy qualitative properties previously used to ground accounts of external memory. Moving forward, we anticipate further work on this subject could reveal principled ways to exploit the environment as a substitute for explicit internal memory.

</details>


### [151] [Hidden in Plain Sight: Visual-to-Symbolic Analytical Solution Inference from Field Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08863)
*Pengze Li,Jiaquan Zhang,Yunbo Long,Xinping Liu,Zhou wenjie,Encheng Su,Zihang Zeng,Jiaqi Liu,Jiyao Liu,Junchi Yu,Lihao Liu,Philip Torr,Shixiang Tang,Aoran Wang,Xi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ViSA-R2模型，用于从二维线性稳态场的可视化图像推断符号解析解，采用自验证的链式推理流程，并在ViSA-Bench基准上优于现有模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从视觉观察中恢复物理场的解析解是AI辅助科学推理的重要但未充分探索的能力，特别是在二维线性稳态场领域

Method: 提出ViSA-R2模型，采用自验证、以解为中心的链式推理流程，模仿物理学家的推理路径：结构模式识别→解族假设→参数推导→一致性验证

Result: 在ViSA-Bench基准（包含30个线性稳态场景）上，使用8B参数的Qwen3-VL骨干网络，ViSA-R2在数值精度、表达式结构相似性和字符级准确率方面优于开源和闭源前沿VLM模型

Conclusion: ViSA-R2展示了从视觉观察推断符号解析解的能力，为AI辅助科学推理开辟了新方向，并提供了可验证的基准和评估协议

Abstract: Recovering analytical solutions of physical fields from visual observations is a fundamental yet underexplored capability for AI-assisted scientific reasoning. We study visual-to-symbolic analytical solution inference (ViSA) for two-dimensional linear steady-state fields: given field visualizations (and first-order derivatives) plus minimal auxiliary metadata, the model must output a single executable SymPy expression with fully instantiated numeric constants. We introduce ViSA-R2 and align it with a self-verifying, solution-centric chain-of-thought pipeline that follows a physicist-like pathway: structural pattern recognition solution-family (ansatz) hypothesis parameter derivation consistency verification. We also release ViSA-Bench, a VLM-ready synthetic benchmark covering 30 linear steady-state scenarios with verifiable analytical/symbolic annotations, and evaluate predictions by numerical accuracy, expression-structure similarity, and character-level accuracy. Using an 8B open-weight Qwen3-VL backbone, ViSA-R2 outperforms strong open-source baselines and the evaluated closed-source frontier VLMs under a standardized protocol.

</details>


### [152] [SPPO: Sequence-Level PPO for Long-Horizon Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08865)
*Tianyi Wang,Yixia Li,Long Li,Yibiao Chen,Shaohan Huang,Yun Chen,Peng Li,Yang Liu,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPPO是一种新的强化学习算法，针对LLM推理任务中的PPO问题，通过序列级上下文赌博机重构和分离的标量值函数，解决了标准PPO在长推理链中的信用分配不稳定和内存成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准token级PPO在LLM推理任务中存在两个主要问题：1）长思维链中的时间信用分配不稳定；2）值模型的内存成本过高。而像GRPO这样的无critic替代方案虽然缓解了这些问题，但需要多个样本进行基线估计，导致计算开销巨大，严重限制了训练吞吐量。

Method: SPPO将推理过程重新构建为序列级上下文赌博机问题，采用分离的标量值函数来推导低方差优势信号，无需多采样。这种方法结合了PPO的样本效率和基于结果更新的稳定性。

Result: 在数学基准测试上的广泛实验表明，SPPO显著超越了标准PPO，并且与计算密集的基于组的方法性能相当，为对齐推理LLM提供了一个资源高效的框架。

Conclusion: SPPO通过序列级优化方法，在保持PPO样本效率的同时解决了长推理链中的稳定性问题，为LLM推理任务的对齐提供了一个可扩展且资源高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) is central to aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) in reasoning tasks with verifiable rewards. However, standard token-level PPO struggles in this setting due to the instability of temporal credit assignment over long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) horizons and the prohibitive memory cost of the value model. While critic-free alternatives like GRPO mitigate these issues, they incur significant computational overhead by requiring multiple samples for baseline estimation, severely limiting training throughput. In this paper, we introduce Sequence-Level PPO (SPPO), a scalable algorithm that harmonizes the sample efficiency of PPO with the stability of outcome-based updates.
